- **html-injection**: HTML injection / content spoofing — unsanitized input rendered as markup without full script execution. Covers dangling-markup data exfiltration, form/link injection (credential theft, action hijack), content spoofing/defacement, and escalation toward XSS. Tier 1. Payloads: payloads/xss.md + client-side.md. Use when input is reflected/stored as HTML but script appears filtered.
